**Missing verification on the actual model.** A joint region analysis;
surviving the witness would still not prove primitive feasibility.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Isolated floating failures become a family
theorem,or a surviving necessary test becomes a seed certificate.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

### Useful geometric family to the original positive theorem
**Input required.** One static legal function per word,all remote and
optimum rows,true minimum/radius,and a uniform quantitative payoff.
**Output claimed.** Eventually the schedule-independent all-n/all-h
lower bound of Section0.
**Available facts.**1252b8823bb5a290 and scoped reference facts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Even a benchmark-strength
unbounded H_m law is absent;the arbitrary-word bridge is separate.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A legal weak trajectory or a diagnostic
word family is mistaken for the full minimax theorem.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "All initial screens pass,so the word is primitive."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.** Actual-function drafts
  14532189ad44824c include screen-passing words with negative primitive
  energy. The full identity in04219554e41d9750 requires a full array.
- "Infeasible solver status excludes the whole family."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** It is a numerical report at a
  fixed word. Only an exact witness or coefficient proof excludes a
  stated region;all eight proposals being bad would still be finite.
- "Full PSD may be replaced by zero remainder or one terminal square."
  **Not automatic.** The original seed interface4c92d3fc4eb6153e has no
  such restriction;old ansatz failures do not decide the full cone.
- "Actual core queries force a quadratic tail."
  **Mathematically incorrect as stated.** acb656c151ded7bf gives a
  later remote-row violation for that continuation of its retained jets.
- "A selected family is weak,so W_n(h) is small."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** W_n is a supremum over all legal
  functions;an upper bound on one witness family is a method limitation.
- "A feasible direction proves a finite capacity gain."
  **Method shortcut,not a proof step.** A finite path must retain every
  nonnegative coefficient and original PSD condition through its endpoint.

Do not retune the two excluded unit families inside their stated bands.
Do not recast the same eight fixed words as a new discovery mechanism.
Do not replace all remote interpolation rows with adjacent checks.
Do not erase moving-normal costs or the old power-range assumptions.
Do not identify zero-step formal gradients in the full certificate.
Do not call the missing arbitrary-word profile bound a routine induction.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1 - an actual full improving seed
**Target statement.** The complete existential lemma in Section0,
including all original variables and the exact capacity inequality.
**Prerequisites.** One actual nonnegative word and a jointly proved array.
**Existing facts to use.**4c92d3fc4eb6153e,04219554e41d9750.
**Missing checks.** Every original sign,flow equation and full PSD.
**Closure criterion.** An exact concrete object satisfies the accepted
lift,followed by independent attack and verifier acceptance. Independent
of the positive lower-bound architecture.

### Bridge2 - finite sign/PSD-preserving schedule deformation
**Target statement.** For one chosen r=5 or6,an explicit finite parameter
interval and h(t),Lambda(t),M(t) satisfying all Section0 constraints
throughout,with a known feasible start and a sufficient-capacity endpoint.
**Prerequisites.** Actual coefficients,not an unknown feasibility oracle;
word and multiplier changes are allowed together.
**Existing facts to use.**04219554e41d9750's full primitive identity.
**Missing checks.** Integration and every boundary/sign/kernel condition.
**Closure criterion.** A readable exact family reaches a concrete endpoint;
an infinitesimal or below-threshold family is partial only. This is one
possible construction strategy dependent on Bridge1's final requirements.

### Bridge3 - global state geometry beyond the weak selected families
**Target statement.** An explicit static construction on every original
H_m in an unbounded range,with original enclosing parameters at every
child,and useful benchmark-scale actual terminal payoff;then an explicit
schedule-independent extension to every large n and every h>=0.
**Prerequisites.** Every physical query,remote/optimum row,true minimum
and actual initial distance;dimension may grow with the entire word.
**Existing facts to use.**1252b8823bb5a290 and complete reference facts;
e9a55f57461f253c is only a finite motivating instance.
**Missing checks.** A useful actual law and the separate arbitrary-word
bridge. Both are substantial mathematical obligations.
**Closure criterion.** A complete quantitative legal law is meaningful
component progress;only the uniform extension resolves the positive
target. Independent of the finite-seed direction.

assumption is transferred to the full cone.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "Verified thresholds resolve the problem." **Conditional only.** The
  actual above-threshold seed is the unfilled hypothesis.
- "Matching plus capacity gives a seed." **Mathematically incorrect as
  a general inference.** C109's word has an actual original-cut rejection.
- "Higher rank repairs any rejected word." **Not automatic.** A full-cone
  static witness excludes every certificate representation at that word.
- "Every boundary term has the right sign." **Mathematically incorrect

**Target statement.** An explicit legal original-H_m construction of

**Target statement.** Prove or refute hereditary interval constraints

# 2. Interface contract table

### Full seed to asymptotic negative resolution
**Input required.** The exact original h,Lambda,M of Section0.
**Output claimed.** A fixed relative improvement at actual N=(r+1)(b*2^j-1) for every sufficiently large j after the strict matched ratio is proved. The explicit N=15*2^j-5,j>=25 statement is ONLY the r=4,b=3,A>=193/25 specialization.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** Actual full seed, not a
necessary-system survivor or below-threshold certificate.
**Failure mode if ignored.** Conditional amplification becomes a false
unconditional original-target disproof.
**Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Actual multistage correlated law to original diagnostic strength
**Input required.** Original H_m with fixed enclosing-m parameters,
every physical query,directed and optimum row,one explicit static
function and its true nearest-minimizer radius. Dimension and active
planes may grow;no incompatible fixed children are presumed composable.
**Output claimed.** A genuinely competitive unbounded-family lower
witness with complete same-word and original-B comparison,followed by
an explicit unmatched all-n/all-h extension.
**Available facts.**Finite actual-H6 e9a55f57461f253c,interpolation
1252b8823bb5a290,and complete reference/benchmark facts.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A concrete new changing
dependence law and its uniform paid comparison;finite H6 does not
transfer when enclosing parameters change. Common lifts are insufficient.
**Failure mode if ignored.** A weak bounded-prefix calibration,unknown
Gram oracle or finite example replaces the original quantitative gap.
**Status.** UNKNOWN.
### Joint covariance to full chronological kernel control
**Input required.** Original physical chronology, full K/T recursions,
constant column, signed killing terms and terminal boundaries.
**Output claimed.** An exact block/martingale factorization that retains
the joint child state, or an exact formal negative direction with its
separate all-Q legality assessment.
**Available facts.**1252b8823bb5a290,b98bb807d514e80d only in their scopes.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A concrete identity closing
the signed margins; positive entries and a stable inverse do not do so.
**Failure mode if ignored.** The old scalar-collapse gap is renamed a
matrix lemma, or a relaxed negative direction is called an actual witness.
**Status.** UNKNOWN.

### Discrete-ray threshold to a stronger actual seed comparison
**Input required.** Original all-split benchmark,integer rays Z_b,full
finite offsets,an actual fixed r and correctly oriented exact enclosure.
**Output claimed.** A strictly better explicit sufficient capacity
threshold for a future length5/6 seed,with its actual substituted horizon.
**Available facts.**04219554e41d9750 supplies general ray enclosures
and the physical substitution,not a globally optimal phase assertion.
**Missing verification on the actual model.** A concrete strictly
improved bound for a fixed integer b;an actual above-threshold seed
is independently still missing. Weak capacities below C_r cannot be
rescued by phase selection.
**Failure mode if ignored.** An upper bound is used as a lower bound,
a finite phase list becomes exhaustive,or a weak seed is relabeled
improving through the same conditional formula.
**Status.** PARTIAL.

The geometric H_c<0 comparison remains a separate OPEN interface with
no proved general cost payment;it is not removed by the scalar-ray route.
# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- "Arbitrary many or variable steps imply arbitrary words."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** The actual optimized policy has a
  complete constant-step obstruction and an exact unit-step boundary.
- "Joint covariance can be discarded without cost."
  **Not automatic.**5f37096a5102c611 identifies this as the scalar
  induction failure. A new block identity must retain and price it.
- "Positive entries plus stable decimation give PSD."
  **Mathematically incorrect as a general inference.**ff8f8f21b222a81b
  leaves signed margins and the constant load unresolved on the actual word.
- "Second-step exclusion also excludes the reversed region."
  **Not automatic.** No primitive reversal or lossless ordering result is available.
- "The independent scalar audit certifies every global reduction."
  **Conflated with a stronger claim.** It explicitly excludes named
  Bernstein/global arithmetic and requires a local repair.

Do not repeat optimal-height tuning, closed safe-angle tuning, positivity-
only kernel arguments, finite-field-to-real-PSD leaps, unaudited domain
reductions or no-seed-to-affirmative-target inference.
